# T3 Code support matrix: t3code v0.1.1 against Mend's gateway

This matrix grades every part of T3 Code that a t3code client can reach through Mend's
`apps/t3-gateway`: each RPC method, each orchestration command, each config, capability and auth
field, each projection field and turn-item kind, and each control in the web client. For every row
that does not work as t3code expects, it says whether the gateway can close the gap alone or Mend
needs a core change, and how large that change is. It is the work list for the 0.36 roadmap item "T3
Code gateway: 100% of T3 Code". `docs/adr/0012-t3code-gateway.md` describes the gateway, and the
docs page `apps/docs/src/content/docs/integrations/t3code.md` tells a person what works today.

## Summary

The matrix compares two pins:

- Mend at `origin/main` 637009289, plus mend#1050 (`t3-gateway/pin-v0.1.1`), which moves the
  vendored t3code contracts to `v0.1.1-preview.20261017.3254`. The section audits read 637009289.
  The rows that #1050 changes are graded as of #1050 and say so in their notes.
- t3code `v0.1.1-preview.20261017.3254` (b2bab46ec), the newest t3code release. The newest nightly,
  `v0.1.1-nightly.20261017.3238`, is its ancestor. The old pin, `v0.0.46-nightly.20261010.2922`
  (bd2346eda), marks what is `NEW` or `CHANGED` in v0.1.1.

Each row has one grade. IMPLEMENTED works as t3code expects. PARTIAL works with a gap a person can
notice, and the row names the gap. MISSING is absent, refused or silent. OUT-OF-MEND is something
only T3's own cloud provides. Every PARTIAL or MISSING row has a scope: `gateway-only` (buildable in
`apps/t3-gateway` over Mend's existing HTTP API, with the routes named), `core` (a Mend engine, API
or store change, sized S for a route over existing engine state, M for new engine behaviour in one
package, L for a cross-package change), or `rule` (Mend refuses it by ADR 0007).

| Section                                  | IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded  | gateway-only | core    | rule  |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ------- | ------------ | ------- | ----- |
| §A RPC methods                           | 40          | 11      | 146     | 2           | 199     | 32           | 122     | 2     |
| §B Orchestration commands                | 18          | 4       | 25      | 0           | 47      | 14           | 15      | 0     |
| §C Config, capabilities, auth            | 64          | 8       | 44      | 2           | 118     | 23           | 27      | 2     |
| §D Projection                            | 64          | 14      | 47      | 0           | 125     | 31           | 30      | 0     |
| §E1 Sidebar, menus, palette, keybindings | 38          | 2       | 27      | 0           | 67      | 18           | 11      | 0     |
| §E2 Composer, thread view, launch        | 32          | 16      | 41      | 0           | 89      | 26           | 30      | 1     |
| §E3 Right panel                          | 20          | 9       | 54      | 1           | 84      | 24           | 39      | 0     |
| §E4 Settings, onboarding, pairing        | 21          | 1       | 33      | 1           | 56      | 11           | 22      | 1     |
| **Total**                                | **297**     | **65**  | **417** | **6**       | **785** | **179**      | **296** | **6** |

The sections overlap on purpose: a UI row in §E and the RPC or field behind it in §A to §D are both
counted, so the total counts rows, not distinct features. Inside a section, each feature has one
row. The 75 rows that graded a feature a second time are listed under their section as
cross-references and are not counted.

### Gaps the gateway can close alone

These 33 features need no Mend core change. Each is sized for one pull request against
`apps/t3-gateway`, and each names the Mend routes it reads or calls.

1. **Pins and thread order.** Per-person pin and order keys in gateway state, as settle and snooze
   are kept, and the `threadPinning`, `threadPinReorder` and `threadActiveReorder` capabilities. No
   Mend route. It also makes sidebar rows draggable, which unblocks drag into Settled and drag onto
   the composer. Rows: B.27, B.28, B.29, C.E13, D.45, D.46, E1a.10, E1a.11, E1a.12, E1a.13, E1a.14,
   E1a.49b (12).
2. **Automatic settlement.** A per-person settle pass in `lifecycle.ts` over Mend turn times
   (`GET /api/sessions/:id/turns`), the per-thread opt-out, the scope and keep-PR settings, and
   stopping a settled thread's session (`POST /api/sessions/:id/stop`). It never deletes a worktree.
   Rows: B.25, B.26, C.E11, C.E26, C.E27, C.S15, D.43, E1a.17, E1a.18 (9).
3. **Steer by interrupt and send (stopgap).** `POST /api/turns/:id/interrupt`, then the message
   first in the queue through `POST /api/sessions/:id/turns`, and
   `supportsSteeringByInterruptRestart`. The interrupted turn loses its in-flight tool work. See the
   steering decision. Rows: B.3, B.12.
4. **Model and service tier from the agent's next start.** The choice kept per thread, as #727 keeps
   effort, then `GET /api/sessions/:id/waiting`, `POST /api/sessions/:id/stop` and
   `POST /api/sessions/:id/launch` with `model` and `speed`. A provider switch through
   `POST /api/sessions/:id/resume` is unverified (E2a.39). Rows: B.35, C.P7, E2a.38, E2a.39, E2a.42
   (5).
5. **A per-person settings home.** Model favorites, hidden models and order (open as mend#1055), the
   environment icon, per-project providers, and the server rows Mend can carry (`GET /api/settings`,
   `PUT /api/settings`, `/api/me/notifications`, `/api/me/git-author`). t3code's `settings:write` is
   all or nothing on the client, so once the gateway grants it, it must serve or refuse every patch
   key by name. Rows: A.29, C.E28, C.S12, E2a.37, E4.2, E4.3, E4.20, E4.N8 (8).
6. **Keybinding edits.** Per-person rules in gateway state, served as `keybindings` with
   `keybindingsUpdated`. "Open keybindings.json" and "Open logs folder" get a refusal that says why.
   No Mend route. Rows: A.23, A.24, C.C4, C.C5, C.C11, E4.7, E4.8, E4.12 (8).
7. **Pull request list and inbox.** `GET /api/projects/:id/pull-requests` and `GET /api/projects`
   behind `pullRequests.list`, `listStats`, `summary`, `routing`, `git.resolvePullRequest` and the
   composer `#` picker, with the `pullRequests` capability. Rows: A.71, A.72, A.73, A.74, A.75,
   A.138, E1b.r62, E3.41, E3.48, E3.49, E3.50, E3.54 (12).
8. **Pull requests on threads.** A session's landings (`GET /api/sessions/:id/landings`,
   `GET /api/changes/:id/landings`, `POST /api/landings/:id/refresh`) as the thread's pull request:
   the sidebar badge, the Linked tab, PR search terms, `thread.pull-request-link-updated` and the
   remote half of the VCS status. Links a person adds are kept in gateway state. Rows: A.77, B.32,
   C.E16, D.20, D.E3, E1a.29, E3.8, E3.NG2, E3.52, E3.56 (10).
9. **Check out a same-repository pull request.** `headRefName` from
   `GET /api/projects/:id/pull-requests`, then `POST /api/projects/:id/refresh`,
   `POST /api/projects/:id/worktrees` with that base, and `POST /api/worktrees/:id/sessions`. A
   fork's head needs a core change (A.139). Row: E3.47.
10. **Push and pull request as a landing.** `POST /api/sessions/:id/land` behind
    `git.runStackedAction` for the change's owner, its report rendered as t3code's result, and
    `GET /api/changes/:id/landings` for the next branch. Anyone else gets Mend's `LandingNotAllowed`
    words. Rows: A.137, E3.43b, E3.43c (3).
11. **Review guide from Mend's tour.** `GET /api/changes/:id/tour` and `POST /api/changes/:id/tour`
    for a pull request Mend landed. It is reachable only once the pull request panel is served.
    Rows: A.82, A.83, E3.NG1 (3).
12. **Add a project by URL or from GitHub.** `POST /api/projects` (adopt), `GET /api/github/status`,
    `GET /api/github/repos`, `GET /api/me/git-access`, a GitHub login through
    `POST /api/me/sealant/accounts` and `DELETE /api/me/sealant/accounts/:id`, a folder browse
    through `GET /api/organization/folders` and `GET /api/organization/folders/:id/files`, and clone
    progress from the gateway's own adoptions. Rows: A.34, A.103, A.104, A.106, A.108, A.119, C.E21,
    E1a.33, E1a.34a, E1a.36, E4.25, E4.27 (12).
13. **Remove a project, delete or create a worktree.** `DELETE /api/projects/:id`,
    `DELETE /api/worktrees/:id` and `POST /api/projects/:id/worktrees`. Two of these wait on a
    decision below. Rows: A.141, A.142, E1a.25, E1a.40 (4).
14. **Provider subagents and Claude workflows.** Claude `Task*` and `task` items and Codex
    `collabAgentToolCall` items from `GET /api/sessions/:id/items` as `subagents[]` with `workflow`,
    and a stop through `POST /api/sessions/:id/waiting/end`. A child transcript needs a core change.
    Rows: A.186, B.40, D.60, D.N4, D.99, D.101, D.E6, E2b.N3, E2b.N4, E2b.47 (10).
15. **Todo checklists.** Claude `TodoWrite` and Codex `plan` and `todoList` items from
    `GET /api/sessions/:id/items` as `plans[]`, `todo_list` items and `plan.updated`. Rows: D.70,
    D.83, D.E9, E2b.48 (4).
16. **Background work.** `GET /api/sessions/:id/waiting` and running `task` items as
    `pendingBackgroundTasks`, and the banner's Stop through `POST /api/sessions/:id/waiting/end`.
    Rows: D.36, D.63, E2b.13 (3).
17. **Projection fidelity.** From `GET /api/sessions/:id/turns` and `GET /api/sessions/:id/items`:
    the context meter at each turn's end, a harness-opened turn as a `notification`, interrupt
    markers, checkpoint items, file-change counts, file-search items, the latest run's error, the
    early-wake stamp, work start times and bounded snapshots. Rows: D.27, D.34, D.44, D.57a, D.64,
    D.69, D.77, D.81, D.85, D.87, D.90, D.91 (12).
18. **Find in thread.** A search over the projection the gateway holds, built from
    `GET /api/sessions/:id/items`, and the `threadFind` config. Rows: A.191, A.192, C.C16, E2b.50
    (4).
19. **Command output streaming.** `orchestration.subscribeCommandOutput` from the item text the hub
    republishes (`GET /api/sessions/:id/items`), and the `commandOutputStreaming` capability. Rows:
    A.199, C.E29, D.E16, E2b.N5 (4).
20. **Inline message context.** `inlineMessageContext`, with thread chips expanded into the turn
    text from `GET /api/sessions/:id/transcript`. Rows: E2a.17, E2a.22, E2a.23 (3).
21. **Skills in the composer.** `GET /api/skills` and `GET /api/projects/:id/skills` as the
    provider's `skills`, with a `workspaceSnapshots` entry per directory so the composer stops
    rescanning. Rows: A.179, C.P23, E2a.30, E2a.31 (4).
22. **Open in an editor over Remote-SSH.** `remoteOpenTargets` from `GET /api/workspace-ssh`. Rows:
    A.118, C.C9, E1b.r50, E3.21 (4).
23. **Repository identity and prompt links.** `originUrl` from `GET /api/projects/:id` as
    `repositoryIdentity`, which also groups one repository across environments. Rows: D.3, E1b.N3,
    E4.N9 (3).
24. **File metadata, fresh listings and absolute paths.** `filesystem.getMetadata`, a listing cache
    dropped when the agent writes, absolute paths mapped to their worktree, and favicon discovery,
    over `GET /api/projects/:id/files` and `GET /api/worktrees/:id/contents`. Rows: A.120, C.C3,
    D.7, E3.24, E3.38, E3.NP3 (6).
25. **Several models at once.** `requiredWorktreeBootstrap`, and one session per model through
    `POST /api/projects/:id/sessions`. Rows: C.E9, E2b.29.
26. **Launch fidelity.** `startFromOrigin` through `POST /api/projects/:id/refresh`, a retry of a
    failed preparation through `POST /api/sessions/:id/launch`, the resume turn without synthetic
    text, up to 100 images per message, signed uploads on the gateway, `thread.create` once plan
    mode exists, and "Local" as a shared worktree if Yiannis picks that reading. Rows: A.195, B.37,
    B.38, B.45, B.47, C.S4, E2a.6, E2a.12, E2a.13, E2b.4, E2b.42 (11).
27. **Worktree setup scripts.** `t3.json` read with `GET /api/worktrees/:id/contents`, run in
    `POST /api/sessions/:id/shell`, its progress published, and cancel through
    `POST /api/processes/:id/stop`. Rows: E2b.6, E2b.70.
28. **Project scripts.** Scripts kept per project in gateway state, run in the shell over `/api/tty`
    with t3code's environment variables, beside Mend's recipes from `GET /api/sessions/:id/recipes`.
    Rows: C.S8, D.8, E1a.41, E2b.61 (4).
29. **Diff and git menu fidelity.** Ignore-whitespace on "Changes" through
    `GET /api/worktrees/:id/diff`, `vcs.refreshStatus` pushing a fresh status, and a no-op pick of
    the thread's own branch. Rows: E2b.57, E3.17, E3.44 (3).
30. **Desktop preview bookkeeping and discovered servers.** Preview sessions kept per thread for the
    Electron app, URLs and local servers from `GET /api/services` and
    `GET /api/sessions/:id/processes`. Rows: A.169, E3.6, E3.57, E3.58, E3.60, E3.61 (6).
31. **Authorized clients.** `GET /api/me/devices`, `DELETE /api/me/devices/:id` and
    `POST /api/me/devices/pairings` behind `subscribeAuthAccess`. Rows: A.181, E4.18.
32. **Provider sign-out.** `GET /api/me/sealant` and `DELETE /api/me/sealant/accounts/:id`. Rows:
    A.14, C.P17.
33. **Environment theme.** Mend's palette on `environmentThemesUpdated`. Row: C.C19.

### Gaps that need a Mend core change

These 64 changes are grouped by the change, not by the row. The size is the change's. A row in the
group may size its own slice smaller.

**Turns and the agent**

- **Native steering (L).** `AgentSession.steer` in `packages/agent-protocol` (Codex `turn/steer`
  with `expectedTurnId`, Claude a stream-json user message with priority `now`),
  `ProtocolHost.steerTurn` in `packages/sessions`, and `POST /api/turns/:id/steer`. Rows: B.2,
  E2a.3, E2a.4, E2a.49 (4).
- **Options on a live agent (M).** `model`, `effort` and `speed` per turn on
  `SubmitAgentTurnRequest`, an in-session permission change, `auto-accept-edits` and `auto` modes,
  and a persistent "always allow". Rows: B.5, B.13, B.22, E2a.40, E2a.41, E2a.43 (6).
- **Provider handoff in one thread (L).** A cross-harness resume in protocol mode, with the
  distilled conversation as the opening turn, recorded as a handoff. Rows: B.36, C.P3, D.74, D.95,
  D.E11 (5).
- **Plan mode (L).** A plan interaction mode at launch and per turn (Claude permission mode `plan`,
  Codex plan collaboration mode) and proposed-plan items in the record. Rows: B.34, C.P4, D.17,
  D.35, D.57c, D.71, D.82, E2a.27, E2a.28, E2b.39 (10).
- **Fork, rollback and merge back (L).** A session forked at a turn's checkpoint with its native
  conversation, a rollback of worktree and conversation to a checkpoint, and a fork's summary
  carried back. Rows: B.41, B.42, B.43, C.P8, D.21, D.48, D.96, D.E10, E2b.40, E2b.41 (10).
- **Delegated tasks (L).** A session that starts and supervises child sessions, with a completion
  mailbox. Rows: B.44, D.57b, D.97 (3).
- **Peer links (L).** Mend as an MCP peer that another environment's agents link to with a pairing
  code, fences and link requests, and Mend as the client of another. Rows: A.30, A.31, A.32, A.33,
  C.E30, D.N1, D.N2, D.N5, D.N6, E1a.55, E2a.24, E2b.N12, E4.N4, E4.50 (14).
- **Usage-limit stops (M).** Adapters classify a failure and `AgentTurn` records the class and the
  reset time, so limit recovery can resume at reset. Rows: B.18, D.28, D.29, D.30, D.93, E2b.72 (6).
- **Live token usage (M).** Codex `thread/tokenUsage/updated` and Claude per-message usage kept on
  the run, and the context window on the turns route. Rows: C.P5, D.67, D.E8, E2a.44 (4).
- **Compaction (M).** `POST /api/sessions/:id/compact` for a live protocol session, and Claude's
  `compact_boundary` recorded as an item. Rows: D.94, E2a.33.
- **Provider slash commands (M).** The harness's command list per workspace and a way to run one.
  Rows: C.P22, E2a.29.
- **Codex goal (M).** The thread goal recorded on the session. Rows: D.38, D.66.
- **Secret requests (M).** An agent-facing secret prompt recorded as a request kind, answered
  without the record keeping the secret. Rows: A.63, D.98, E2b.73 (3).
- **MCP apps (L).** MCP tool calls with UI resources on Mend's items, and a proxy to the app's
  server. Rows: A.127, A.128, A.129, A.130, E2b.46 (5).
- **Workflow scripts (L).** Claude workflow files kept where `getWorkflowScript` can read them.
  Rows: A.185, E2b.49.
- **HTML render tool (M).** An agent-facing render tool whose output is an item with an HTML
  attachment. Row: E3.NP5.
- **Restart continuation (M).** A turn cut by a Mend restart runs again after it. Whether the engine
  already does this was not checked. Row: E4.6.
- **Thread titles on demand (S).** `POST /api/sessions/:id/label/generate` over the existing namer.
  Rows: B.16, C.E14, D.13, E1a.19 (4).
- **Text generation (M).** A route that answers t3code's writing asks with Mend's inference on the
  asker's own login. Rows: C.P9, E4.4.

**Files, worktrees and git**

- **Raw bytes of a worktree file (S).** `GET /api/worktrees/:id/raw?path=&at=` with content type, a
  size cap and HTTP Range. It unblocks images, HTML, PDF, video and audio previews, markdown images
  and favicons. Rows: A.123, E3.29, E3.30, E3.31, E3.32, E3.33, E3.34, E3.NP4, E3.59 (9).
- **Reads at the project's default branch (S).** A file read and a content search over the bare
  store, for draft threads and the project root. Rows: A.111, A.112, E3.27 (3).
- **Listing pages (S).** Paging and directory-scoped listing in `GET /api/projects/:id/files`. Rows:
  A.110, A.113.
- **File writes (L).** `PUT /api/worktrees/:id/contents` for the change's owner or under shared
  control, captured workspaces included. Rows: A.116, E3.28, E3.35 (3).
- **Non-image attachments (M).** Any file placed in the workspace beside images, on messages and on
  question answers. Rows: A.125, A.126, B.6, C.E7, E2a.14, E2a.15, E2a.16, E2a.19 (8).
- **Working-tree status (M).** HEAD, index, uncommitted files and upstream ahead and behind over the
  API, and the uncommitted diff. Capture-backed sessions make it L. Rows: A.132, A.136, A.146,
  E3.13, E3.NG3 (5).
- **Diff against a project ref (S).** A project ref as `from` on `GET /api/worktrees/:id/diff`. Row:
  E3.16.
- **Commit (M).** `POST /api/worktrees/:id/commit` as the person. Row: E3.43a.
- **Pull (M).** `POST /api/worktrees/:id/pull`: fetch, then fast-forward or merge into the worktree.
  Rows: A.135, E3.43d, E3.43e (3).
- **Branch switch and create in a session's worktree (L).** A store `checkoutBranch` and
  `POST /api/sessions/:id/branch` for the owner. Waits on a decision. Rows: A.143, A.144, B.17,
  E3.43g, E3.NG4 (5).
- **A pull request's head from a fork (M).** Fetch `refs/pull/N/head` into the store and base a
  worktree on it. Row: A.139.
- **Publish a repository (M).** `gh repo create` and a push of the default branch as the person.
  Rows: A.105, E3.45.
- **Worktree setup feed (S).** The install run at workspace start as a progress feed, stoppable.
  Rows: A.133, A.134.
- **Storage cleanup and worktree retention (M).** A report and a run in `packages/store`, with the
  keep-when guards from the working-tree status. Rows: A.25, C.E18, C.S11, E4.9, E4.N2 (5).
- **Editor launch on Mend's machine (S).** Real `availableEditors`, useful only when that machine
  has a display. Row: E3.37.

**Projects**

- **Scratch projects and new projects by name (M).** A store method that creates an empty repository
  with a first commit as a private project, and its routes. Rows: A.114, A.115, C.C18, E1a.28,
  E1a.35 (5).
- **Add a project from a host path (L).** Adoption from a directory on the Mend host and a host
  listing route. Waits on a decision. Rows: E1a.31, E1a.32.
- **"Local" mode (L).** A session in the project checkout. Waits on a decision. Rows: D.2, E2b.3.
- **Project rename and icon (S).** `PUT /api/projects/:id/name` and icon fields with a route. Rows:
  A.117, E1a.37, E1a.38 (3).
- **Project defaults (M).** A per-project default model and runtime mode. Row: E1a.43.
- **Other forges (M).** GitLab, Forgejo, Bitbucket and Azure providers beside `gh`. Row: E1a.34b.
- **Cancel an adoption (S).** Stop a clone in flight. Row: A.107.
- **Import agent sessions (L).** Scan the person's Codex and Claude history and import it as
  projects and sessions. Rows: A.122, E4.45.

**Pull requests**

- **Pull request read model (L).** Detail, checks, timeline, review threads, files, candidates,
  viewed state and a refresh feed, read as the person over GitHub. Rows: A.76, A.78, A.79, A.80,
  A.81, A.84, A.85, A.86, A.96, A.97, A.98, A.99, A.101, E2a.18, E3.7, E3.9, E3.51, E3.55 (18).
- **Pull request writes (L).** Comment, review, reply, resolve, react, reviewers, labels and viewed
  files through the person's `gh`. Rows: A.87, A.90, A.91, A.92, A.93, A.94, A.95, A.100, A.102 (9).
- **Pull request watch (M).** A worker that refreshes a landing's pull request and wakes the agent
  on a failed check, a review or a comment. Rows: B.33, E3.53.

**Search, usage and limits**

- **Message search (M).** `GET /api/sessions/search` over the person's session items. Rows: A.190,
  E1a.30.
- **Usage summary (M).** Tokens and cost per harness and model from `agent_turns.usage`, with a
  price table. Rows: A.53, A.54, C.E19, C.S10, E3.67, E3.68, E3.71 (7).
- **Limits (M).** Rate-limit windows read through each person's harness login (Codex
  `account/rateLimits`, Claude limit events). Spending a reset credit makes it L. Rows: A.5, C.C20,
  C.P25, E2a.35, E3.69, E3.70, E4.40 (7).

**Harnesses and logins**

- **Provider sign-in (L).** A login flow Mend runs as the person (start, complete, respond, cancel,
  progress), stored where `mend connect` stores it. Rows: A.6, A.7, A.8, A.9, A.10, A.11, A.12,
  A.13, A.15, C.P10, E4.36, E4.38, E4.47, E4.59 (14).
- **Login details (S).** The account email, plan and login kind on `GET /api/me/sealant`. Rows:
  C.P16, E2a.45.
- **Harness configuration (M).** Per-person enable, display name, environment and custom models.
  Rows: A.4, E4.35, E4.48 (3).
- **Harness install and update (L).** The planned auto-updating harness feature, with versions and
  advisories. Rows: A.16, A.17, A.18, A.19, C.P13, C.P26, E4.39 (7).
- **More harnesses (L).** pi and opencode as protocol sessions, and an ACP registry. Rows: A.35,
  A.36, A.37, A.38, A.39, A.40, A.41, A.42, A.43, A.44, A.45, C.C7, E4.37 (13).
- **Feedback upload (M).** `/feedback` run in the session's workspace on the person's login. Rows:
  A.131, E2a.34.

**Instance and operations**

- **Preview browser (L).** A server-side browser per session streamed to the client, and agent
  browser tools. Rows: A.158, A.159, A.160, A.161, A.162, A.163, A.164, A.165, A.166, A.167, C.E22,
  E2b.63, E3.5, E3.NP6, E4.23 (15).
- **Device hub (L).** iOS simulators and Android emulators per host. Rows: A.170, A.171, A.172,
  A.173, A.174, A.175, A.176, A.177, E3.10, E3.62, E3.63, E3.64, E4.24 (13).
- **Scheduled sessions (L).** Cron, interval and webhook triggers on pg-boss, a webhook ingress and
  delivery logs. Rows: A.58, A.59, A.60, A.61, A.62, A.64, A.65, E4.32 (8).
- **Server diagnostics (M).** Traces, process tree and resource telemetry over the API, and a signal
  for a listed process. Rows: A.46, A.47, A.49, A.50, A.51, A.55, A.183, E3.75, E4.10, E4.11 (10).
- **Host resources (S).** CPU and memory on the API. Row: A.48.
- **Telemetry export (M).** OTLP endpoints for Mend's server, operator only. Rows: A.52, C.E25,
  E4.N3 (3).
- **Server self-update (L).** A route that runs `mend server upgrade` with progress. Rows: A.20,
  A.21, C.E20 (3).
- **Landing writing policy (M).** Branch naming and commit and pull request text per person. Row:
  C.S13.
- **Review guide settings (M).** t3code's guide settings served from Mend's machine review pass.
  Row: C.S14.
- **A per-person settings model (M).** For the remaining server settings Mend adopts, one per
  behaviour. Row: C.S17.

### Out of Mend

These 6 rows need T3's own cloud. Mend cannot provide them, however large the work.

- A.69 `cloud.getRelayClientStatus` and A.70 `cloud.installRelayClient`: the T3 Connect relay is
  T3's cloud service.
- C.E23 `agentActivityPublishing`: it publishes agent activity to T3 Connect.
- C.E31 `linkRevoked`: it is a T3 Connect account link.
- E3.66 Connect onboarding, relay install and remote environment list: T3 Connect is T3's relay and
  Clerk accounts.
- E4.22 the T3 Connect switch, activity publishing and webhook hold: T3's relay and account service.

Peer links (E4.N4, D.N6) are not on this list. They pair over the peer's own `/mcp` URL, so Mend
could serve them itself.

### Refused by rule

ADR 0007 refuses these, and the gateway renders each refusal the way t3code renders a missing
permission.

- **Merge, auto-merge and stack merge** (A.88, C.E17, E2b.N10). Mend never merges. t3code leaves an
  action out when it is absent from `viewerPermissions.actions` and every `mergeCapabilities` flag
  is false. The stack controls stay inert with t3code's "lacks permission" tooltip while
  `source-control:write` is withheld.
- **Rebase of a pushed branch** (A.89). It is a force-push, and Mend never force-pushes. t3code
  hides an update method absent from `viewerPermissions.updateMethods`.
- **Merge settings** (C.S16, E4.26). "Merge method" and "Remove agent credits on merge" render inert
  with "This connection lacks permission to change settings on <gateway>." while `settings:write` is
  withheld.
- **A push or pull request by anyone but the change's owner** (inside A.137, E3.43b and E3.43c,
  which are counted gateway-only for the owner). Scopes are per connection, so the control is live
  on a teammate's thread. The refusal is t3code's "Git action failed" toast carrying Mend's
  `LandingNotAllowed` message. A draft pull request is ADR 0007's open question 3.

Branch rows are not on this list. The git audit read ADR 0007 decision 11 ("Mend never moves the
session's branch") as a rule for E3.NG4 and E3.43g. The matrix grades them as a core change that
waits on decision 10, because decision 11 covers landing's own writes.

## Decisions for Yiannis

Each decision names the rows it settles. A recommendation is the author's reading of the evidence,
not a verdict.

1. **Steering: native, or interrupt and send.** The stopgap (gateway feature 3) makes Ctrl+Enter,
   the "steer" follow-up setting and the queue's Steer button work now, and costs the interrupted
   turn's in-flight tool work. Moving the 0.37 "Steering in Mend" item into 0.36 takes four pieces:
   an engine steer op (Codex `turn/steer {expectedTurnId}`, Claude a stream-json user message with
   priority `now`), `ProtocolHost.steerTurn` in `packages/sessions`, `POST /api/turns/:id/steer`,
   and the gateway mapping `steer_active` to it with `supportsActiveSteering`. Rows: B.2, B.3, B.12,
   E2a.3, E2a.4, E2a.49. Recommendation: ship the stopgap in 0.36 and map `steer_active` to it as
   well, then land native steering as one L stack. Only the turn's own sender may steer it, because
   a steer spends that turn's login.
2. **Model, effort and provider changes on a started thread.** Model and tier from the agent's next
   start are gateway-only (feature 4), as effort already is since #727. A change on the live agent
   is core M, and a provider switch is core L. Recommendation: build feature 4 for 0.36 and leave
   live changes and provider handoff for 0.37.
3. **Plan mode.** It is core L: a plan interaction mode for both harnesses at launch and per turn,
   proposed-plan items, and their follow-ups. ADR 0012 lists it as "never". Recommendation: take it
   into 0.37 and revise the ADR row now, so the matrix and the ADR agree.
4. **Fork, rollback and merge back.** Checkpoints make a fork at a turn possible without touching
   the source worktree. An in-place "Edit from here" rewrites files the owner holds. E2b.40 names a
   lossy gateway-only stand-in: a new session opened with the transcript. Recommendation: build fork
   first (no work is lost), then rollback that checkpoints before it resets, then merge back.
5. **Delegated tasks and peer links.** Both are L and need Mend's agents to get an orchestrator MCP.
   Peer links are not OUT-OF-MEND: they run peer to peer over `/mcp`. Recommendation: defer both to
   0.38 and keep the capabilities absent, which t3code reads as unsupported.
6. **The raw worktree file read route.** One S route, `GET /api/worktrees/:id/raw?path=&at=`,
   unblocks 9 media and favicon rows. Recommendation: build it in 0.36.
7. **Usage and limits.** Mend records `agent_turns.usage`, so a usage summary is M. Limits read
   through each person's own login are M, and spending a reset credit is L. Recommendation: usage
   summary and limits in 0.36.1, each spent on the asker's own login. Reset credits later.
8. **Scratch projects and new projects by name.** One store method creates an empty repository with
   a first commit as a private project (M), and it unblocks 5 rows. It fits ADR 0003, because the
   repository lives in Mend's store. Recommendation: build it.
9. **Add a project from a host path.** ADR 0003 replaced host mounts with Mend-managed folders, and
   adopting a host path brings them back (L). Recommendation: keep host paths refused. Serve the
   browse over organization folders (gateway feature 12), and add "adopt from a folder" only if
   folders become adoptable.
10. **Branch switch in a session worktree.** ADR 0007 decision 11 says "Mend never moves the
    session's branch", and the decision log ties it to landing's commit-tree writes. B.17 reads it
    as allowing an owner's own checkout. E3.NG4 and E3.43g read it as a rule. Recommendation: keep
    switch and create refused for 0.36 (t3code already renders them inert), and decide with an ADR
    0007 amendment whether the owner may switch when the worktree has one session and no landing in
    flight.
11. **Delete the worktree too, and remove a project, from t3code.** Both routes exist and guard
    themselves: `DELETE /api/worktrees/:id` refuses while a session is live or the change is not on
    origin, and `DELETE /api/projects/:id` checks `canRemoveProject`. Recommendation: allow both
    through the gateway (E1a.25, E1a.40), behind t3code's own confirm dialogs.
12. **"Local" environment mode.** t3code always offers "Local" first, and today it fails after send
    (E2b.3). It can mean a session in the project checkout (core L, against "every session runs in
    one worktree") or a per-project shared worktree on the default branch (gateway-only, B.47).
    Recommendation: the shared worktree, labelled in the notice the gateway already shows.
13. **ADR 0012's "never" row.** The row (`docs/adr/0012-t3code-gateway.md:129`) lists steering, plan
    mode, fork, rollback, delegated tasks, PR watch, preview, devices, usage and more as "never".
    This matrix grades them MISSING with a scope. Recommendation: split the row into "refused by
    rule" (merge, force-push, a non-owner landing), "out of Mend" (T3 Connect) and "not yet", with
    this matrix as the list.

## §A. RPC methods

Each row is one method of `WsRpcGroup` at v0.1.1: 199 methods, the pin's 188 plus 11 new, none
removed or renamed. #1050 registers the 11 new methods with typed answers. The t3code column cites
`packages/contracts/src/rpc.ts` at v0.1.1 and the method's scope. "Withheld" means the gateway does
not grant the scope: `apps/t3-gateway/src/auth.ts:73-79` grants `orchestration:read`,
`orchestration:operate`, `terminal:read`, `terminal:operate` and `filesystem:read`. The Gateway
column cites `apps/t3-gateway/src/rpc.ts` at 637009289, or names the answer #1050 gives. `web` is
t3code's `apps/web/src`. A feed that honestly answers "none" is IMPLEMENTED, and so is a method no
v0.1.1 client sends.

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- |
| 40          | 11      | 146     | 2           | 199         | 0                |

Scope of the 157 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 32 gateway-only, 122 core (S 11, M 27, L 84), 2 rule, and
1 graded per command in §B.

## §B. Orchestration commands

The client union `OrchestrationV2Command` has 55 types at v0.1.1; the one addition is
`subagent.stop`. The 47 rows group them, and each reaches the gateway through
`orchestration.dispatchCommand` (A.184). `N:` is t3code's
`packages/contracts/src/orchestrationV2.ts` at v0.1.1 and `P:` the same file at the pin. `cmd:`,
`launch:` and `proj:` are the gateway's `commands.ts`, `launch.ts` and `thread-projection.ts`, and
`cr:` is t3code's `packages/client-runtime/src/operations/commands.ts`. Every core row starts from
Mend's `AgentSession` (`packages/agent-protocol/src/types.ts:125-153`): it has `sendTurn`,
`interrupt`, `respond`, `respondInput`, `quiescence`, `endWork` and `close`, and nothing that
steers, changes an option on a live agent, plans, forks or rolls back.

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- |
| 18          | 4       | 25      | 0           | 47          | 0                |

Scope of the 29 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 14 gateway-only, 15 core (S 2, M 6, L 7), 0 rule.

## §C. Config, capability and auth fields

`v011:` and `pin:` name the t3code tree. `C/` is `packages/contracts/src`, `CR/` is
`packages/client-runtime/src`, `W/` is `apps/web/src` and `S/` is `apps/server/src`. Bare file names
are gateway files in `apps/t3-gateway/src`. A field the client does not read, or one whose absence
is right for a headless Mend host, is IMPLEMENTED with that reason.

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- |

## §D. Projection fields and turn-item kinds

`packages/client-runtime/src`. Gateway paths are `apps/t3-gateway/src` unless a Mend package is
named. The projection schema version is 2 in both t3code trees and in the gateway.

## §E. The web client

The E rows are controls in t3code's v0.1.1 web client. Each section names its own path keys.

### §E1. Sidebar, menus, palette and keybindings

In E1a, a bare path is t3code's `apps/web/src/`, `cr/` is `packages/client-runtime/src/`, `ct/` is
`packages/contracts/src/`, `G/` is Mend's `apps/t3-gateway/src/` and `A/` is Mend's
`packages/api-contracts/src/`. In E1b, `N/` and `O/` are `apps/web/src/` at v0.1.1 and at the pin,
`Nc/` and `Ncr/` are v0.1.1's contracts and client runtime, and an `r` number is a row of the
2026-10-11 audit's E1.

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- |
| 38          | 2       | 27      | 0           | 67          | 32               |

Scope of the 29 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 18 gateway-only, 11 core (S 3, M 5, L 3), 0 rule.

### §E2. Composer, thread view and launch

In E2a, a client path is under t3code's `apps/web/src/components/` unless prefixed, `CR/` is
`packages/client-runtime/src` and `G/` is the gateway. In E2b, a client path is under
`apps/web/src/components/` unless it starts with `packages/`, `hooks/` or `client-runtime/`, and a
bracketed number is a row of the 2026-10-11 audit's E2. Two E2b entry points are graded in their own
slices and are not rows here: the thread actions menu (the E1a menu rows) and thread automations
(E4.32).

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- |
| 32          | 16      | 41      | 0           | 89          | 22               |

Scope of the 57 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 26 gateway-only, 30 core (S 4, M 15, L 11), 1 rule.

### §E3. Right panel

`N:`, `NCR:`, `NC:` and `NS:` are t3code's web app, client runtime, contracts and server at v0.1.1,
and `O:`, `OCR:` and `OC:` the same at the pin. `G:` is the gateway, `MC:` is Mend's
`packages/api-contracts/src`, `ML:` is `packages/landing/src`, `MS:` is `packages/store/src` and
`MA:` is `apps/api/src`. E3.NG rows are new in the git part and E3.NP rows new in the panel part.

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- |
| 20          | 9       | 54      | 1           | 84          | 8                |

Scope of the 63 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 24 gateway-only, 39 core (S 11, M 13, L 15), 0 rule.

#### Diff, git and pull requests

##### Right panel: Diff and pull-request tabs

Gates in v0.1.1: Diff needs `isServerThread && isGitRepo` (N:components/ChatView.tsx:3745,
`isGitRepo` from `vcs.isRepo` :3148). Every PR tab reads `capabilities.pullRequests` /
`threadPullRequests` (N:components/RightPanelTabs.tsx:718-735), both absent
(G:environment.ts:78-84). The shell sets only `branch` and `worktreePath` (G:shell.ts:386-387,
615-616), never `linkedPullRequest`/`pullRequests`/`branchPullRequest`, and `vcs.pr` is null
(G:vcs.ts:162).

##### GitActionsControl and the branch toolbar

Only mount: N:components/chat/ThreadDetailsPanel.tsx:148-172 (needs `gitCwd`,
N:ChatView.tsx:3034-3039; `BranchToolbar layout="panel"` when `isGitRepo`, `GitActionsControl` when
the project has a name). O:ThreadDetailsPanel.tsx:147-170. Every write in it is gated on
`source-control:write` (`canWriteSourceControl`, N:GitActionsControl.tsx:974-977;
N:BranchToolbarBranchSelector.tsx:116, 159), which #727 withholds (G:auth.ts:73-79, 82-85; stored
grants narrowed at G:auth.ts:115-122), so the main button is disabled with the reason "This
connection cannot change source control." (N:GitActionsControl.tsx:1144-1148, :1757), every menu
item is disabled (:1585), the commit dialog's Commit and the default-ref Continue are disabled
(:2011, :2057), and a call that slips through fails client-side with the same words
(N:state/sourceControlActions.ts:93-105). The status-derived hints (`hasWorkingTreeChanges:false`,
ahead/behind 0, `pr:null`, G:vcs.ts:56-69, 156-165) decide the label underneath: "Push" + "No local
commits to push." with origin (N:GitActionsControl.logic.ts:287-301). Logic (`.logic.ts`), selector
and control are functionally identical to the pin (cdiff: comment-only changes).

##### Pull requests

Code: `N:routes/_chat.pull-requests.tsx`, `N:components/pullRequest/**`. Every entry reads
`capabilities.pullRequests` / `threadPullRequests` / `threadPullRequestLinking` /
`threadPullRequestWatch` / `pullRequestStackActions` / `pullRequestChecks` (NC:environment.ts:84-85,
126-130), all absent, so no `pullRequests.*` RPC fires; the gateway's typed failures
(G:rpc.ts:779-819) are defence in depth. The PR _writes_ are also client-guarded on
`source-control:write` (NC:clientRpcPermissions.ts:17-27), withheld. Mend's own PR facts come from
landing (ADR 0007): `ChangePullRequest` per change (number, url, state, title, head branch, observed
time, `adopted`), refreshed only on demand (`POST /api/landings/:id/refresh`, owner only) because
"Mend does not poll GitHub".

## Method and evidence

Section auditors read Mend at 637009289 and both t3code trees, and wrote one file per section:
`A-rpc.md`, `B-commands.md`, `C-config.md`, `D-projection.md`, and the E parts `E1a.md`, `E1b.md`,
`E2a.md`, `E2b.md`, `part-git.md`, `part-panel.md` and `part-settings.md`. They live in
`~/.claude/orchestrate/t3-gateway-full/matrix/` on the build machine, with full `file:line`
citations in both trees. Section A built its rows from `WsRpcGroup` with scripts that resolve every
tag in both trees, diff each `Rpc.make` block with comments stripped, and diff the scope maps.

This document was built from those files by scripts in `/tmp/t3matrix/` on the same machine:

1. `extract.mjs` parses every table row of the eleven files. `ids.mjs` gives each row its id (E3's
   new rows became E3.NG in the git part and E3.NP in the panel part, because both parts numbered
   them N1 onward).
2. `overrides.mjs` applies three kinds of correction. Rows #1050 changes, graded as of #1050: A.27,
   A.30, A.31, A.32, A.33, A.52, A.82, A.83, A.117, A.120, A.186, A.199, B.17, B.40, C.A14, C.A15,
   C.E3, C.S12, D.N3, E1a.20, E1b.N5, E1b.r61, E2a.16, E2a.41, E2b.N3, E2b.N4, E2b.N5, E2b.N12,
   E3.NG1, E3.NP3, E4.6, E4.N1, E4.N3, E4.N4, E4.21, E4.N5. Rows whose scope or grade two auditors
   disagreed on, or whose named route is not in `packages/api-contracts`, reconciled against the
   cited code: C.E26, A.186, B.17, C.P7, E2b.47, E2b.N4, E3.NG4, E3.43g, C.S8, C.S4, E2b.39, E3.43e,
   E4.53. And 75 cross-references, one row per feature.
3. `groups.mjs` and `check.mjs` put every counted PARTIAL and MISSING row into exactly one gateway
   feature or core change above, and fail on a row in none or in two. `routes.mjs` checks that every
   Mend route a gateway-only scope or feature names is an endpoint in `packages/api-contracts`.
4. `counts.mjs` computes every count and row list in this document. The method and type totals in §A
   and §B come from the section audits' own scripts.
5. Agents shortened each section's cells to fit this page, and `validate.mjs` held them to the
   source: the same ids and grades, the same scope class and core size, and the `NEW`, `CHANGED` and
   `#1050` markers kept. The section audits keep the full evidence.
6. `build.mjs` renders this file. `pnpm format:fix` formats it.

What was read and what was observed:

- Every row was graded from source, in Mend and in both t3code trees. No auditor ran a server.
- #1050's verification observed the gateway live: t3code's hosted web app at both tags, driven by
  playwright-core against the gateway and a fake Mend, through pairing, the sidebar, opening and
  launching threads, a follow-up, the diff panel and the model star. It also decoded the new gateway
  with the old pin's own schemas. The matrix takes the model-favorites regression (C.S12, E2a.37)
  from that run.
- Rows carried from the 2026-10-11 audit keep its live spot checks where the auditors confirmed the
  code is unchanged.
- A few rows say what they could not confirm: E2a.39 (whether a resume keeps the session id), E4.6
  (whether Mend's engine resumes a turn after a restart), and the claims about Effect Schema
  ignoring unknown keys, which #1050's old-schema decode test now covers.
